Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in modular theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 295 and the invariant I(A,B) = 110 mod 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_295(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 295. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^110(S, F) is trivial. QED.
